# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-05)

## Corpus Check
- 7684 files · ~17,709,088 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 544 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 402, (none) 42, .css 18)

## Summary
- 75880 nodes · 221923 edges · 1665 communities (1498 shown, 167 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9460 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `ad2ea052`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
